MA	<p>7d26_6a01</p> <p>Restriction enzymes are proteins that cut DNA at specific sequences. <span style="color: #00775f;"><i>ZraI</i></span> is derived from Zoogloea ramigera 11, and <span style="color: #0067cc;"><i>AluI</i></span> is derived from Arthrobacter luteus. Both enzymes are labeled on the map.</p><p><strong>DNA Fragment Question:</strong> Shown below is a short DNA fragment that is only 10 kb in length.
